Implementation plan detailed:

```
def next_pow2(m):
    p=1
    while p<m: p <<=1
    return max(p,8)
```
Need N for convolution of length n and len 2n-1: n+(2n-1)-1=3n-2. For self conv length 2n-1 N>=2n-1; using N=3n-2 okay.

Function `fft_self(a, N)` returns A, c, max_c. Use N=next_pow2(3n-2) for all for simplicity. To reduce memory, maybe if N large > 65536? cap. For n=100000, N=262144? still memory 2MB. But time. We'll limit n 20000.

```
def linear_convolution_fft(x, y, N=None):
   lenx=len(x); leny=len(y); if N is None: N=next_pow2(lenx+leny-1)
   return np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(x,N)*np.fft.rfft(y,N), N)[:lenx+leny-1]
```
For many self, use specialized.

Function `offset_weights(c, p, topK, alpha, uniform_frac, rng=None)`:
```
L=len(c); cmax=max(c)
if cmax<=0: q uniform
r=c/cmax
poww=np.power(r,p)
if poww sum==0: one-hot argmax
poww/=sum
if topK>1: q_top zeros; idx=np.argpartition(-c, min(K,L)-1)[:K]; q_top[idx]=1/K
q=alpha poww + (1-alpha)q_top
q += 1e-3/L
q/=sum
```
Be careful argpartition for K=1. Use `np.argpartition(-c, k-1)[:k]` if k>0. For negative? c nonneg.

Function `grad_weights_self(a,q,N,A=None)`:
- L=2n-1, q_rev=q[::-1]
- if A is None: A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
- else use A. But N maybe chosen for 3n-2; A length N/2+1. q_rev length L. QR=np.fft.rfft(q_rev,N); conv=np.fft.irfft(A*QR,N); return 2*conv[2n-2:3n-2], A maybe.
Check slice indices for L-1=2n-2, length n -> stop 3n-2. If N length enough 3n-2? Need N>=n+L-1=3n-2? conv result indices 0..3n-3; stop 3n-2 exclusive. N must be >=3n-2. next_pow2(3*n-2) returns >=.

For cross derivative b: no factor2. Use same function with factor=1.

Function `node_adam_state()`? Since no closures, pass lists? We'll manage state in run_search.

```
def adam_update(g, w, m, v, t, lr, clip=5.0):
   t+=1; b1=0.9; b2=0.999
   m = b1*m + (1-b1)*g
   ...
   dw = mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+1e-12)
   dw = np.clip(dw,-clip,clip)
   w +=? Wait gradient descent: w_new = w - lr*dw. But if g is gradient w.r.t w. Our glog = a*(grad - avg). Need minimize: w_new = w - lr*dw.
   recenter w_new max 0
   a=softmax(w_new)
   return a,w_new,m,v,t
```
But note if we use glog as gradient, moving w - lr*normalized gradient. For cross b, gradient wrt w_b = b*(g - avg). Minimize H -> w_b <- w_b - lr*... Good.

Resampling methods:
```
def resample_sequence(a, m):
   n=len(a)
   if m==n: return normalize(a)
   if m < n:
      blocks = n//m
      use=m*blocks; out=mean(a[:use].reshape(m,blocks), axis=1) if blocks>1 else a[:m]
      # or downsample by stride m_n? linear interp maybe better:
   else:
      # linear interpolation from n to m using coordinates 0..n-1, normalize
      x=np.linspace(0, n-1, m); out=np.interp(x, np.arange(n), a)
   # ensure nonnegative, normalize
```

Time budget: We can implement a robust loop:
```
while time < deadline:
  # self gradient smooth step(s)
  for s in range(self_steps):
    c, score = self_conv(a)
    y = offset_weights(c,...)
    g = 2*conv(a,y_rev)
    glog = a*(g - dot(a,g))
    a,w,m,v,t = adam(glog,w,m,v,t,lr_self)
    evaluate if improves
  # b subproblem
  for _ in range(b_steps):
    h = cross(a,b)
    y = offset_weights(h)
    g = conv(a,y_rev)
    glog = b*(g-dot(b,g))
    b,w_b,... = adam(...)
    if hmax < b_best: update
  # combine a,b with line search
  best_mix = ...
  a = best_mix
  # restarts
```

Direct gradient code:
```
def direct_top_grad(a, offsets, factor):
   n=len(a); g=np.zeros(n)
   for k in offsets:
      k=int(k)
      lo = 0 if k < n else k - n + 1
      hi = k if k < n else n - 1
      if lo > hi: continue
      # slice indices
      start = k - lo
      stop = k - hi - 1
      g[lo:hi+1] += a[start:stop:-1]
   return g * factor
```
Check for k=0: lo=0,hi=0,start=0,stop=-1 -> slice a[0:-1:-1] yields empty? In Python, a[0:-1:-1] with stop -1 excludes index? For start 0, stop -1, step -1 gives [] because -1 is not less? Need care. For length1, want a[0]. Use if length? Better use flip small slice or index assignment reversed.

General: lo=max(0,k-n+1), hi=min(k,n-1). j_min=k-hi, j_max=k-lo. Slice a[j_min : j_max+1][::-1]. Add.

`grad_top_diff(a, idx, w, factor)`:

for idx_k, wk in zip(idx,w):
   k=int(idx_k)
   lo = 0 if k < n else k - n + 1
   hi = k if k < n else n - 1
   if lo <= hi:

```

def make_smooth_noise(n, rng, max_freq=80, amp=0.1):
   idx=np.arange(n)
   t=np.arange(n)/float(n)
   noise=np.zeros(n)
   for m in range(1,max_freq+1):
      phase=rng.uniform(0,2π)
      amp = amp / (m**1.0)
      noise += amp*np.cos(2π*m*t + phase)
   return noise
```

Define resample:
```
def resample_linear(a,m):
   n=len(a)
   if m==n: return a
   x_out=np.linspace(0,n-1,m)
   b=np.interp(x_out, np.arange(n), a)
   return normalize(b)
def resample_tile(a,m):
   n=len(a)
   if m<n:
       # downsample by stride with average? use np.resize? Better linear average:
       return resample_linear(a,m)
   reps = -(-m//n)
   tiled=np.tile(a, reps)
   if len(tiled)>m:
       # interpolate down from tiled length to m for smooth transition
       tiled=np.interp(np.linspace(0,len(tiled)-1,m), np.arange(len(tiled)), tiled)
   else: # reps*m < m? reps ceil so enough
   return normalize(tiled)
```
Tile-repeat with trim can create discontinuity at join? Tile seamless; trim at end discontinuity. Interpolating down from tiled length to m smooths wrap? If trimming, `l = reps*n >=m`. Interp from l to m samples within one period. Good.
Take better of linear and tile by initial FFT score. For m multiple n, tile exact repeat. For m smaller, linear average may be better.

Let's design `run_optimizer` in more concrete code:

```
def run_optimizer(a0, budget, seed=0, verbose=True):
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max(0,budget-2)
    n=len(a0)
    a=normalize(a0)
    w=log_weights(a)
    Nscore=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    score,c = fft_score_c(a,Nscore)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score
    b=a.copy(); wb=log_weights(b); b_H=score? (hmax for a*b maybe score? Actually h max maybe <=score? For uniform same. compute later)
    base_eta=0.03; base_eta_b=0.05
    base_K = max(4, int(0.002*n)); max_K=min(300, int(0.02*n)+20)
    p=2.0
    no_imp=0; it=0; last_print=0
    b_steps_base=2
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it +=1
       # if current invalid reset
       if not finite... continue
       # self gradient line search
       c, score = fft_score_c(a,Nscore) # c maybe from candidate? We didn't store c after previous; recompute
       if score < best_score: update best
       # choose K
       K = min(max_K, base_K + no_imp//20)
       # maybe trival K from [1, K, 3,5,10,20,50]
       # gradient
       idx, weights = topK_weights(c,K,p,rng)
       g = direct_grad_self(a, idx, weights)
       d1 = n*(g - np.mean(g))
       # maybe clip d to [-10,10]

       accepted=False

           try score. If <cur_score:
               accept; base_eta = min(0.5, base_eta*1.05); no_imp=0; accepted=True; break

       else:
           # try natural gradient? maybe
           glog = n*a*(g - np.dot(a,g))
           for eta in (base_eta, ...): ... if accept
           if not accept: no_imp += 1; base_eta=max(0.001, base_eta*0.7)
       # b subproblem every iteration? or every self step
       # Maybe run b for b_count = 2 + no_imp//100
       for _ in range(b_count):
          if time low break
          h = fft_conv_ab(a,b,Nscore)
          hm = max(h)
          if hm < b_H: b_H=hm; b_best=b.copy()
          idx_h, wh = topK_weights(h, min(K,20), p, rng)
          g_b = direct_grad_cross(a, idx_h, wh)
          d_b = n*(g_b - np.mean(g_b)); clip
          accepted_b=False
          for eta in (base_eta_b, ...):
             wb_try=wb - eta*d_b; b_try=softmax; h_try=fft_conv(a,b_try); hm_try=max
             if hm_try < hm:
                accept; base_eta_b=...; accepted_b=True; break

       # combine with b_best or b

          cand = mix_linear(a,b,t) or logmix

       if accepted mix: a=...; w=...; no_imp=0; else no_imp +=1
       # if no_imp large, mutate

       # periodic print
```

`fft_score(a, N=None, return_c=False)`:
```
n=len(a)
if N is None: N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)
c=c[:2*n-1]
if c.size:
   # clip negative
   if np.any(c<0): c=np.maximum(c,0)
   mx=float(c.max()) if c.size else 0
else: mx=0
score=2*n*mx
if return_c: return score,c
return score
```

```

`direct_top_grad(a, idx, weights, factor)` as above. `idx` int array, weights float same. Need if weights sum? We normalize in topK weights. For factor, multiply at end or loop. Multiplying each shift by wk*factor maybe. We'll multiply g by factor after.

```
def direct_top_grad(a, idx, weights, factor=1.0):
    n=len(a); g=np.zeros(n,float)
    for kk, wk in zip(idx, weights):
       if wk ==0: continue
       k=int(kk)
       if k < 0: continue
       L=2*n-1
       if k >= L: continue
       lo = 0 if k < n else k - n + 1
       hi = k if k < n else n - 1
       if lo > hi: continue
       j_min = k - hi
       j_max = k - lo
       # j_min>=0, j_max<n
       seg = a[j_min:j_max+1][::-1]
       g[lo:hi+1] += wk * seg
    if factor !=1.0: g *= factor
    return g
```
Check for k=n-1: lo=0 hi=n-1, j_min=0? k-hi=0, j_max=n-1, seg a[0:n][::-1]; g[0:n] += a_rev. Good.
For k=2n-2: lo=n-1 hi=n-1, j_min=n-1, j_max=n-1, seg [a[n-1]]; g[n-1]+=w*a[n-1]. Good.

`topK_weights(c,K,p, rng=None)`:
```
L=len(c)
K=min(max(1,int(K)),L)
if K==1:
   idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))]); weights=np.array([1.0])
else:
   # If c has NaNs? handled before.
   part=np.argpartition(-c, K-1)[:K]
   # maybe sort by c desc for stable? not needed
   vals=c[part]
   # if p==0 uniform top; else power
   vals=np.maximum(vals,0)
   if p <= 0: w=np.ones(K)/K
   else:
      # scale to max to avoid underflow
      vmax=vals.max()
      if vmax <=0:
          idx onehot argmax c
      else:
          r=vals/vmax
          w=np.power(r,p)
          s=w.sum()
          if not finite or s<=0: onehot
          else w/=s
   idx=part
```
If p large and many ties? OK. If K > L, uniform.

Maybe add randomness: With probability, drop highest? For diversity. Could implement: if rng and K>1 and rng.random()<0.2: use K2 = max(1,K//2) plus one random offset? But topK deterministic may converge too fast. Randomizing K and p enough. In run loop choose K from options: [1,2,3,5,10,20,40,80] depending no_improve. Instead of fixed K, each iteration select `K = int(rng.choice(K_options, p=...))`. This stochasticity helps. But for smooth convergence, use median. Let's use schedule with random choice from `[1, 2, 3, 5, 8, Kbase, Kbase*2]` with probabilities. `K_options = [1,2,3,5,8,16,32,64,128]` filtered <=max_K. We can choose K randomly; topK weights p=2-5.

For b subproblem, K_b smaller maybe `min(K, 20)`.

`smooth_noise(n, rng, amp, max_m)`:
```
t=np.arange(n)/float(n)
noise=np.zeros(n)
mmax = min(max_m, n//2)
for m in range(1,mmax+1):
    coeff = amp / (float(m)**0.8)
    phase = rng.uniform(0,2*math.pi)
    noise += coeff*np.cos(2*math.pi*m*t + phase)
return noise
```

       for bb in range(b_count):
           if time... break

`resample_tile(a,m)` for m < n uses linear downsample. For m>n, tile. Need if a len=0.

```
def resample_tile(a,m):
   n=len(a)
   if n==0: return normalize(np.ones(m))
   if m==n: return normalize(a)
   if m<n:
      return np.interp(np.linspace(0,n-1,m), np.arange(n), a)
   reps=-(-m//n)
   timed=np.tile(a,reps)
   if len(timed)>m:
      timed=np.interp(np.linspace(0,len(timed)-1,m), np.arange(len(timed)), timed)
   # if len< m (shouldn't) pad with last
   out=normalize(timed)
   return out
```
For m not multiple, trim and interpolate from len(timed) to m. Good.

Block shuffle function with tail:
```
def random_block_shuffle(a, rng, block_size):
   n=len(a)
   if block_size <=1 or n <= block_size:
      return a.copy()
   out=np.empty(n,float)
   i=0
   # create chunks list
   chunks=[]
   while i<n:
      e=min(n,i+block_size)
      chunks.append((i,e))
      i=e
   order=list(range(len(chunks))); rng.shuffle(order)
   for p in order:
      s,e=chunks[p]
      out[s:e]=a[s:e]
   # This moves whole chunks but values assigned to their original slots according to permuted chunk content? Actually if we assign content of chunk p to slot s,e where s,e are current p's original range? We loop p in order and assign to p's original range; that permutes contents among ranges. Good. But we overwrite out with original positions; no interference.
```
We need sort? not. Tail smaller.

if frac<=0: return normalize(a)

threshold=np.sort(a)[-k]  # keep top k? Actually set bottom frac to 0: threshold percentile 100(1-frac). Use np.partition(a, n-k)[n-k].
out=np.where(a > threshold, a, 0.0)
if out.sum()==0: out=np.ones_like(a)
return normalize(out)
```

```
def mix_linear(a,b,t):
   c=(1-t)*a + t*b
   s=c.sum()
   if s>0 and abs(s-1)>1e-12: c=c/s
   return c
```

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    try budget=float(budget_s) except: 2400
    budget=max(1,min(budget,2390))
    t0=time.monotonic()
    parent=load_parent()
    if parent is None: parent=default_parent(4096,seed=seed)
    max_n=16000
    if parent.size > max_n: parent=resample_linear(parent, max_n)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    cand_items=generate_candidates(parent, max_n, rng)
    # choose up to 3
    selected=select_items(cand_items, max_n)
    total=budget-30
    results=[]
    if len(selected)>0:
       if len(selected)==1: fracs=[1.0]
       elif len(selected)==2: fracs=[0.7,0.3]
       else: fracs=[0.62,0.24,0.14]
       for i,item in enumerate(selected):
           l.item arr=...; b=fracs[i]*total
           if b < 5: continue
           res_arr,res_score=run_optimizer(arr,b,seed=seed+i,verbose=(i==0))
           results.append((res_arr,res_score))
    # fallback if none
    # exact validate top results
    final_candidates=[]
    for arr,sc in results:
        if arr.size>MAX_EXACT_N:
            # maybe downsample? no, final can be large? If >25000 maybe too slow exact, but we limited max_n=16000.
        val=exact_score_norm(arr)
        final_candidates.append((val,arr,sc))
    choose min exact or FFT if exact inf.
    final=finalize_sequence(chosen)
    return final
```

Let's implement no momentum, just RMS:
```
def rms_step(d, v):
   beta=0.9
   d=np.where(finite,d,0)
   v=beta*v + (1-beta)*d*d
   denom=np.sqrt(v)+1e-8
   step=d/denom
   step=np.clip(step,-5,5)
   return step, v
```
Then w_try = w - eta*step.

`try_mutation`:
```
def try_mutation(current_a, current_w, current_score, N, n, rng, strength):
    best_a=current_a.copy(); best_w=regularize_w(current_w); best_s=current_score
    tested=0
    # smooth noise
    for sig in (0.01,0.03,0.1):
       max_m = min(80, n//4)
       noise=make_smooth_noise(n,rng,sig*max(1,strength),max_m)
       wc=regularize_w(current_w + noise)
       ac=softmax_weights(wc)
       sc=fft_score_only(ac,N)
       if sc < best_s - 1e-12: best...
    if strength >=1:
       # shuffle
       for block in (50,150,500):
          if block < n//2:
             ac=random_block_shuffle(current_a,rng,block)
             sc=...
       # sparsify
       for frac in (0.02,0.1):
          ac=sparsify(current_a,frac)
       # power
       for p in (0.5,2.0):
          base=np.maximum(current_a,1e-300)
          ac=normalize(np.power(base,p))
    return best_a,best_w,best_s,best_s < current_score - 1e-12
```

Function `next_pow2`:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1: return 8
    p=8
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p
```

`normalize`:
```
def normalize(a):
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size==0:
       return np.ones(1,float)
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr),arr,0.0)
    arr=np.maximum(arr,0.0)
    s=float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
       return np.ones(arr.size,float)/float(arr.size)
    return arr/s
```

```

`regularize_w`:
```
def regularize_w(w):
   w=np.asarray(w,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   w=np.where(np.isfinite(w),w,-700.0)
   if w.size:
      mx=float(np.max(w))
      if np.isfinite(mx):
         w=w-mx
      w=np.clip(w,-700.0,100.0)
   return w
```
If w empty? return.

`fft_score_c`:
```
def fft_score_c(a,N=None):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   n=a.size
   if n==0: return np.inf, np.array([0.0])
   if N is None: N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)
   length=2*n-1
   c=c[:length]
   if c.size:
      np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
      mx=float(np.max(c))
   else: mx=0.0
   if not np.isfinite(mx): return np.inf,c
   return float(2.0*n*mx), c
```
`fft_score_only` call.

`fft_conv_ab`:

def fft_conv_ab(a,b,N=None):
   a=...; b=...
   n=len(a); if len(b)!=n? We assume both same. If not, length len(a)+len(b)-1 maybe. But in run both same n. For safety, get length.
   N = next_pow2(len(a)+len(b)-1)
   A=rffta; B=rfftb; c=irfft; length=len(a)+len(b)-1; c=c[:length]; clip; return c
```

`topK_weights`:
```
def topK_weights(c,K,p):
   L=len(c)
   if L==0: return np.zeros(0,int), np.zeros(0,float)
   K=int(min(max(1,K),L))
   if K==1:
      idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))],dtype=np.int64)
      return idx,np.array([1.0])
   part=np.argpartition(-c, K-1)[:K]
   vals=c[part]
   vals=np.where(np.isfinite(vals),vals,0.0)
   vmax=float(np.max(vals))
   if not np.isfinite(vmax) or vmax <=0:
       imax=int(np.argmax(c))
       return np.array([imax],dtype=int), np.array([1.0])
   if p <=0:
      w=np.ones(K,float)
   else:
      r=vals/vmax
      w=np.power(r,p)
   s=float(w.sum())
   if not finite or s<=0:
      imax=int(np.argmax(c)); return onehot
   w=w/s
   return part.astype(np.int64), w
```
If `c` contains NaNs, argmax weird; but c clipped.

`direct_top_grad`:
```
def direct_top_grad(a, idx, weights, factor):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   n=len(a)
   g=np.zeros(n,float)
   L=2*n-1
   K=min(len(idx),len(weights))
   for ii in range(K):
      wk=float(weights[ii])
      if wk == 0.0: continue
      k=int(idx[ii])
      if k < 0 or k >= L: continue
      if k < n:
         lo=0; hi=k
      else:
         lo=k-n+1; hi=n-1
      if lo > hi: continue
      start=k-lo
      stop_incl=k-hi
      if stop_incl > 0:
         seg=a[start:stop_incl-1:-1]
      else:
         seg=a[start::-1]
      g[lo:hi+1] += wk * seg
   if factor != 1.0:
      g *= factor
   return g
```
Potential issue: If start:stop step -1 length mismatch with hi-lo+1? It should. Let's test n=5,k=3: lo=0,hi=3,start=3,stop=0 -> stop>0? 0>0 false -> a[3::-1] length4, g length4. Good. k=4: lo=0 hi=4 start=4 stop=0 -> a[4::-1] length5. k=6 (n=5,L=9): lo=2 hi=4, start=6-2=4, stop=6-4=2 -> stop>0: a[4:1:-1] indices 4,3,2 length3, g[2:5]. Good.

`rms_step`:
```
def rms_step(d,v):
   d=np.where(np.isfinite(d),d,0.0)
   beta=0.9
   v = beta*v + (1-beta)*d*d
   denom=np.sqrt(v)+1e-8
   step=d/denom
   step=np.where(np.isfinite(step),step,0.0)
   step=np.clip(step,-10.0,10.0)
   return step,v
```

`sparsify`:
```
def sparsify(a,frac):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); n=a.size
   if frac <=0: return normalize(a)
   k=max(1, int(np.floor(frac*n)))
   if k >= n: return normalize(a)
   threshold=np.partition(a, n-k)[n-k]
   out=np.where(a > threshold + 1e-300, a, 0.0) # keep top maybe strict; if all equal, none, fallback
   s=out.sum()
   if s<=0:
      out=np.ones(n,float)
   else: out=out/s
   return out
```
Using strict > could drop ties at threshold; fine.

`resample_linear`:
```
def resample_linear(a,m):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); n=len(a)
   if m <=0: return np.ones(1)
   if n==m: return normalize(a)
   if n==1: return np.ones(m)/m? if a[0] positive uniform? return full a[0]/m
   x=np.linspace(0,n-1,m)
   b=np.interp(x, np.arange(n), a)
   return normalize(b)
```
If parent has nonpositive? normalize.

`resample_tile`:
```
def resample_tile(a,m):
   a=normalize(a); n=len(a)
   if m<=0: return a
   if m==n or n==1: return np.ones(m)/m? If a not uniform, tiling single maybe repeat a[0]? use np.resize? If n=1, return np.full(m,a[0]/m)
   if m < n:
      return resample_linear(a,m)
   reps = -(-m//n)
   tiled=np.tile(a,reps)
   if len(tiled)>m:
      x=np.linspace(0,len(tiled)-1,m)
      tiled=np.interp(x, np.arange(len(tiled)), tiled)
   elif len(tiled)<m:
      pad=np.full(m-len(tiled), tiled[-1])
      tiled=np.concatenate([tiled,pad])
   return normalize(tiled)
```

   arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr),arr,0); arr=np.maximum(arr,0)
   s=arr.sum()
   if not finite or s<=0: return None
   return arr/s

   if n0 <= max_n: base_lengths.append(n0)

`select_candidates`:

def select_candidates(items,max_n,k=3):
   if not items: return []
   items_sorted=sorted(items, key=by_item_score)
   chosen=[]
   for item in items_sorted:
      if len(chosen)>=k: break
      # ensure len not duplicate
      if all(item[1] != c[1] for c in chosen): chosen.append(item)
   # add largest length if score close to best
   if len(chosen) < k and items:
      worst_best = min(by_item_score(c) for c in chosen)? cannot use gen? Use loop.
      best_sc=chosen[0][0]
      largest=max(items, key=by_item_len)
      if largest[1] not in [c[1] for c in chosen] and by_item_score(largest) <= best_sc + 0.05:
          chosen.append(largest)
   # add second if missing maybe
   for item in items_sorted:
      if len(chosen)>=k: break
      if all(item[1] != c[1] for c in chosen): chosen.append(item)
   return chosen
```

`try_mutation`: Needs RNG. If strength 0? not. Top-level:
```
def try_mutation(a,w,score,N,n,rng,strength):
   best_a=a.copy(); best_w=regularize_w(w); best_s=score
   # noise sig based on strength
   sigs=(0.01,0.03,0.08) if strength<2 else (0.02,0.06,0.15,0.3)
   max_m=min(100,n//4) if n>4 else 1
   for sig in sigs:
      noise=make_smooth_noise(n,rng,sig,max_m)
      wc=regularize_w(best_w + noise) # use current best_w? Maybe use original w? If previous noise improved, best_w updated. Good.
      ac=softmax_weights(wc)
      sc=fft_score_only(ac,N)
      if sc < best_s - 1e-12: best_a=ac; best_w=wc; best_s=sc
   if strength >=1:
      for block in (50,150,400):
         if block >0 and n > block:
            ac=random_block_shuffle(a,rng,block)
            sc=fft_score_only(ac,N)
            if sc < best_s -1e-12: best_a=ac.copy(); best_w=log_weights(ac); best_s=sc
      for frac in (0.02,0.1):
         ac=sparsify(a,frac)
         ...
      for p in (0.5,2.0,4.0):
         base=np.maximum(a, EPS)
         ac=normalize(np.power(base,p))
         ...
   return best_a,best_w,best_s,best_s < score - 1e-12
```
Using best_w for noise sequentially may accumulate. Starting from current best. Good.
If a and best_a differ after shuffle, noise from current w? We update best_w. Good.

`run_optimizer` now. We must be careful with arrays and reassign.

Pseudo code with all details:
```
def run_optimizer(a0,budget,seed=0,verbose=False):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    if budget <= 2.0:
       a=normalize(a0); s, c = fft_score_c(a, next_pow2(2*len(a)-1))
       return a, s
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, float(budget) - 6.0)
    a=normalize(a0)
    n=a.size
    if n < 8:
       a=normalize(np.ones(4096)); n=4096
    Nscore=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    score,c=fft_score_c(a,Nscore)
    if not finite score: a=ones; score,c=...
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score
    w=log_weights(a)
    b=a.copy(); wb=log_weights(b)
    v_self=np.zeros(n,float); v_b=np.zeros(n,float)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    base_eta=0.05; base_eta_b=0.08
    no=0; it=0; last_print=t0; last_mutation = -100
    Klist = build...
    Kb_list = [1,3,5,8,16,32]
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it +=1
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 4: break
       # validate
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)) or float(a.sum()) <= 0:
          a=best.copy(); score=best_score; w=log_weights(a); v_self.fill(0); continue
       score,c = fft_score_c(a,Nscore)
       if not finite: a=best;...
       if score < best_score - 1e-12: best_score=score; best=a.copy()
       if score > best_score + 0.02:
          a=best.copy(); score=best_score; w=log_weights(a); v_self.fill(0); b=a.copy(); wb=log_weights(b); v_b.fill(0); continue
       improved=False
       # K
       if no < 20:
          K = int(rng.choice([Klist[-1], Klist[-2]]))
       elif no < 100:
          K = int(rng.choice(Klist[-3:]))
       else:
          K = int(rng.choice(Klist))
       p = 1.0 + min(6.0, float(no)/50.0)
       idx,wts = topK_weights(c,K,p)
       if idx.size:
          g=direct_top_grad(a,idx,wts,2.0)
          if g.size:
             mean_g=float(np.mean(g))
             d=n*(g-mean_g)
             d=np.where(np.isfinite(d),d,0.0)
             step,v_self = rms_step(d,v_self)
             alphas=(base_eta, base_eta*0.5)
             if no > 20: alphas = alphas + (base_eta*2.0,)
             best_cand=None; best_wc=None; best_sc=score
             for alpha in alphas:
                wc=regularize_w(w - alpha*step)
                ac=softmax_weights(wc)
                sc=fft_score_only(ac,Nscore)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_sc=sc; best_cand=ac.copy(); best_wc=wc.copy()
             if best_cand is not None:
                a=best_cand; w=best_wc; score=best_sc
                if score < best_score - 1e-12: best_score=score; best=a.copy()
                improved=True; no=0
                base_eta=min(0.5, base_eta*1.08)
                # maybe decay v? v_self*0.7?
       if not improved:
          base_eta=max(0.002, base_eta*0.85)
       # cross update, every iteration?
       if time.monotonic() < deadline - 10:
          if not finite b or sum <=0: b=a.copy(); wb=log_weights(b)
          h=fft_conv_ab(a,b,Nscore)
          if h.size:
             np.maximum(h,0,out=h)
             hmax=float(np.max(h)) if h.size else 0
          else: hmax=0
          if not finite hmax: hmax=0
          if no <20: Kb=int(rng.choice([min(32,Klist[-1]), min(16,Klist[-2])]))? Need Kb_list
          else Kb=int(rng.choice(Kb_list))
          idxb,wtb=topK_weights(h,Kb,p)
          cross_improved=False
          if idxb.size:
             gb=direct_top_grad(a,idxb,wtb,1.0)
             mean_gb=float(np.mean(gb))
             db=n*(gb-mean_gb)
             db=finite
             stepb,v_b=rms_step(db,v_b)
             alphas_b=(base_eta_b, base_eta_b*0.5)
             best_bc=None; best_wbc=None; best_h=hmax
             for alpha in alphas_b:
                wbc=regularize_w(wb - alpha*stepb)
                bc=softmax_weights(wbc)
                hc=fft_conv_ab(a,bc,Nscore)
                if hc.size:
                   np.maximum(hc,0,out=hc); hm=float(np.max(hc))
                else: hm=0
                if finite hm and hm < best_h - 1e-12:
                   best_h=hm; best_bc=bc.copy(); best_wbc=wbc.copy()
             if best_bc is None and no > 10:
                # FW
                jmin=int(np.argmin(np.where(np.isfinite(gb), gb, np.inf)))
                for lam in (0.5,1.0):
                   bc=b.copy(); bc *= (1.0-lam); bc[jmin] = max(0.0, bc[jmin] + lam); s=bc.sum(); if s>0: bc=bc/s
                   hc=fft_conv_ab(a,bc,Nscore); hm=max
                   if hm < best_h -1e-12:
                      best_h=hm; best_bc=bc.copy(); best_wbc=log_weights(best_bc)
             if best_bc is not None:
                b=best_bc; wb=best_wbc; cross_improved=True
                base_eta_b=min(0.5, base_eta_b*1.08)
             else:
                base_eta_b=max(0.002, base_eta_b*0.85)
       # mix
       if time.monotonic() < deadline - 10:
          mix_improved=False
          # compute best mix candidates
          cand_a=None; cand_w=None; best_mix_score=score
          # linear t 0.3,0.7
          for tval in (0.3,0.7):
             ca=(1.0-tval)*a + tval*b; s=ca.sum(); if s>0 and abs(s-1)>1e-12: ca=ca/s
             sc=fft_score_only(ca,Nscore)
             if sc < best_mix_score -1e-12:
                best_mix_score=sc; cand_a=ca; cand_w=log_weights(ca)
          # log t 0.5 and 1.0 if stuck
          for tval in (0.5, 1.0 if no>30 else -1.0):
             if tval <=0: continue
             wc=regularize_w((1.0-tval)*w + tval*wb)
             ca=softmax_weights(wc)
             sc=fft_score_only(ca,Nscore)
             if sc < best_mix_score -1e-12:
                best_mix_score=sc; cand_a=ca; cand_w=wc
          if cand_a is not None and best_mix_score < score -1e-12:
             a=cand_a; w=cand_w; score=best_mix_score
             if score < best_score -1e-12: best_score=score; best=a.copy()
             improved=True; no=0
             # decay v? 
             base_eta=min(0.5, base_eta*1.03)
       if not improved:
          no += 1
       # mutation
       if no > 50 and (no % 50 == 0 or no > 400):
          if score > best_score + 0.005:
             a=best.copy(); score=best_score; w=log_weights(a); v_self.fill(0); b=a.copy(); wb=log_weights(b); v_b.fill(0); no=25; continue
          strength = 1 if no < 200 else 2
          new_a,new_w,new_s,mut_ok=try_mutation(a,w,score,Nscore,n,rng,strength)
          if mut_ok:
             a=new_a; w=new_w; score=new_s
             if score < best_score -1e-12: best_score=score; best=a.copy()
             no=0; improved=True
             v_self.fill(0); v_b.fill(0); b=a.copy(); wb=log_weights(b)
          elif no > 400:
             a=best.copy(); score=best_score; w=log_weights(a); b=a.copy(); wb=log_weights(b); v_self.fill(0); v_b.fill(0); no=0
       # print
       if verbose and time.monotonic() - last_print > 20:
          print(f"[opt] n={n} it={it} current={score:.6f} best={best_score:.6f} no={no} time={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}")
          last_print=time.monotonic()
    # final small exact? not here
    return best, best_score
```

def exact_score_norm(a):
   a=normalize(a); n=a.size
   if n > 20000: # avoid too slow? But search max 16000. 
      # use FFT approximate
      return fft_score_only(a, next_pow2(2*n-1))
   b=np.convolve(a,a)
   mx=float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0
   val=2*n*mx
   return val if finite else inf
```

`finalize_sequence`:
```
def finalize_sequence(a):
   a=normalize(a); n=a.size
   scale=float(np.sqrt(2.0*n))
   a=a*scale
   np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0,out=a)
   s=float(a.sum())
   if not finite or s <=0:
      a=np.ones(n); a*=scale/float(n)
   else:
      a=a*(scale/s)
   np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0,out=a)
   s2=float(a.sum())
   if s2 < 0.01:
      a=a*(max(0.01,s2)/s2?)
   return [float(x) for x in a]
```
If scale/n large? n max 16000 scale=179, avg 0.011, safe. If after clip, sum maybe less, renorm.

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
   try: budget=float(budget_s)
   except: budget=2400.0
   budget=min(max(1.0,budget),2390.0)
   t0=time.monotonic()
   parent=load_parent()
   if parent is None:
       parent=default_parent(4096, int(seed))
   max_n=16000
   if parent.size > max_n:
       parent=resample_linear(parent, max_n)
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   items=generate_candidates(parent, max_n, int(seed))
   selected=select_candidates(items, max_n, 3)
   if not selected:
       a=parent if parent.size<=max_n else resample_linear(parent,4096)
       seq=finalize_sequence(a)
       return seq
   total=max(1.0,budget-40.0)
   if len(selected)==1: fracs=[1.0]
   elif len(selected)==2: fracs=[0.72,0.28]
   else: fracs=[0.62,0.26,0.12]
   results=[]
   for i,item in enumerate(selected):
       sc,l,arr=item
       btime=fracs[i]*total
       if btime < 5: continue
       if time.monotonic() - t0 > budget - 20: break
       print(f"phase {i+1} length={l} initial_score={sc:.6f} budget={btime:.1f}")
       best_arr,best_sc=run_optimizer(arr,btime,seed=int(seed)+i*101,verbose=(i==0))
       if best_arr is not None:
          results.append((best_arr,best_sc,l))
   # if no results, use selected[0] arr
   if not results: results=[(selected[0][2], selected[0][0], selected[0][1])]
   # exact evaluate
   best_final=None; best_exact=inf
   for arr,sc,l in results:
       # skip duplicate lengths? if same length and score higher? We can keep all.
       val=exact_score_norm(arr)
       if np.isfinite(val):
           if val < best_exact:
              best_exact=val; best_final=arr
       else:
           if sc < best_exact:
              best_exact=sc; best_final=arr
   if best_final is None:
       best_final=results[0][0]
   # maybe if exact val > 1.5027 and time remains, run a short polish on best_final? Could if best_exact > 1.5027 and remaining>120:
   remaining=budget - (time.monotonic()-t0)
   if remaining > 60 and best_exact > 1.5027:
       # run one more short optimization on best_final to try polish
       polish_arr,polish_sc=run_optimizer(normalize(best_final), min(remaining-10, 300), seed=int(seed)+999, verbose=False)
       val=exact_score_norm(polish_arr)
       if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_exact:
           best_final=polish_arr; best_exact=val
   seq=finalize_sequence(best_final)
   return seq

Define top-level `lbfgs_smooth_obj(w, n, N, beta_frac)` returns value and grad.
```
def lbfgs_smooth_obj(w, n, N, beta_frac):
    a=softmax_weights(w)
    score,c=fft_score_c(a,N)
    cmax=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0
    if cmax<=0:
       return 0, np.zeros(n)
    beta=beta_frac/cmax
    # for stability c-cmax <=0
    expt = np.exp(np.clip(beta*(c-cmax), -745, 0))
    s=expt.sum()
    q=expt/s if s>0 else uniform/onehot
    # grad with full q using FFT
    q_rev=q[::-1]
    # Nscore currently next 2n-1, but q conv with a length n + L-1 requires N>=3n-2 if using rfft q. Wait if q length 2n-1, conv length 3n-2. Our Nscore is only 2n-1; cannot compute cross via FFT with q full at Nscore. We can use direct full O(n^2)? too slow. Or use direct convolution via np.convolve? q dense length 2n-1, np.convolve O(n^2). Not good. Could use Ngrad=next_pow2(3n-2) in L-BFGS. But objective c uses Nscore; gradient use Ngrad. We can allocate Ngrad. But in function, if N is Ngrad? It can compute c with Ngrad too; larger but okay. pass N = next_pow2(3n-2) maybe. Score c length 2n-1 from Ngrad (valid). Use same N for both.
    A=rfft(a,N); c=irfft(A*A,N)[:2n-1]
    ...
    Q=rfft(q_rev,N); gfull=irfft(A*Q,N)[2n-2:3n-2]
    g=2*gfull
    grad_w = a*(n? gradient wrt w). L-BFGS in w unconstrained, gradient of S wrt w = a*(g - dot(a,g)). Scale arbitrary. Maybe multiply by n for magnitude? Objective values ~ score ~1.5. Gradient scale? g ~1/n, a*(diff)~1/n^2, very small. L-BFGS may struggle. Multiply by n*n? We can choose parameter `grad_scale = float(n*n)`? Since objective J=2n max c ~1.5, derivative wrt w likely O(1/n)? Let's estimate: moving w by 1 shifts a_i factor e, changes score O? For a spread, changing one weight O(1/n) can change max O(1/n). So derivative O(1/n), not 1/n^2? Let's compute g for q delta active: g_i =2a_{k-i} ~2/n. dot~? diff~1/n, a*diff~1/n^2. But w_i change 1 multiplies a_i by e, relative change 1; derivative of score wrt w_i when only i changes should be a_i*(2a_{k-i}-avg) ~1/n^2? Maybe score change small because total mass fixed and one coordinate small. To change score significantly need many coordinates. So gradient magnitude 1/n^2 for n=5000 ~4e-8. L-BFGS will use finite diff? It uses gradient; can step huge because gradient tiny. scaling by n^2 to O1. But objective value ~1, gradient wrt scaled w? If we return grad_scaled = n*n*a*(g-dot), then L-BFGS step w ~? It assumes grad of objective; scaling gradient changes optimization (penalty). We should not scale unless factor constant. def power_weights_all(c,p):
   if c.size==0: return np.zeros(0)
   cmax=float(np.max(c))
   if cmax <=0 or not finite:
      q=np.zeros(c.size); q[int(np.argmax(c)) if c.size else 0]=1; return q
   r=c/cmax
   w=np.power(r,p)
   s=float(w.sum())
   if not finite or s<=0:
      q=np.zeros(c.size); q[int(np.argmax(c))]=1; return q
   return w/s
```

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative coefficient sequences minimizing

    2 * n * max(np.convolve(a, a)) / (sum(a)^2)

by working in the scale-invariant normalization sum(a) = 1.

The main optimization is a time-budgeted stochastic local search:
  - FFT is used to evaluate self- and cross-convolutions,
  - the active constraints are estimated by weighting high convolution offsets,
  - direct top-offset gradients are used for very cheap multiplicative/log moves,
  - an occasional full FFT-weighted gradient is used to smooth over many active offsets,
  - a convex cross-correlation subproblem is approximately solved for a second
    sequence b, and the current sequence is mixed with b,
  - random smooth noise, block shuffling, sparsification, and power transformations
    are used to escape local traps,
  - several candidate sequence lengths are tried from the supplied parent
    construction,
  - final candidates are validated with exact np.convolve.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

EPS = 1e-300
INF = float("inf")


def next_pow2(m):
    """Smallest power of two at least m, with a modest minimum."""
    if m <= 1:
        return 8
    p = 8
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def normalize(a):
    """Return a finite nonnegative vector normalized to sum 1."""
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(arr.size)
    return arr / s


def regularize_w(w):
    """Center and clip log-weights so softmax is stable."""
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if w.size == 0:
        return w
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, -700.0)
    mx = float(np.max(w))
    if np.isfinite(mx):
        w = w - mx
    w = np.clip(w, -700.0, 100.0)
    return w


def softmax_weights(w):
    """Stable softmax to a probability vector."""
    w = regularize_w(w)
    if w.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    e = np.exp(w)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(w.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(w.size)
    return e / s


def log_weights(a):
    """Stable log of a positive probability vector."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, EPS)
    w = np.log(a)
    return regularize_w(w)


def fft_score_c(a, N=None):
    """Return (objective, full self-convolution) for normalized a."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return INF, np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if N is None:
        N = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)
    length = 2 * n - 1
    c = c[:length]
    if c.size:
        np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
        mx = float(np.max(c))
    else:
        mx = 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return INF, c
    return float(2.0 * n * mx), c


def fft_score_only(a, N=None):
    """Objective only."""
    if N is None:
        s, _ = fft_score_c(a)
        return s
    s, _ = fft_score_c(a, N)
    return s


def fft_conv_ab(a, b, N=None):
    """Linear convolution a*b for equal-length sequences."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0 or b.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if N is None:
        N = next_pow2(a.size + b.size - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    B = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * B, N)
    c = c[:a.size + b.size - 1]
    if c.size:
        np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def by_item_score(item):
    return item[0]


def by_item_len(item):
    return item[1]


def topK_weights(c, K, p):
    """Weights over the top K offsets of c, proportional to c^p."""
    L = int(c.size)
    if L == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.int64), np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    K = int(min(max(1, K), L))
    if K == 1:
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        return np.array([idx], dtype=np.int64), np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)

    part = np.argpartition(-c, K - 1)[:K]
    vals = np.asarray(c[part], dtype=np.float64)
    vals = np.where(np.isfinite(vals), vals, 0.0)
    vmax = float(np.max(vals))
    if not np.isfinite(vmax) or vmax <= 0.0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        return np.array([idx], dtype=np.int64), np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)

    if p <= 0.0:
        w = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        r = vals / vmax
        w = np.power(r, float(p))

    s = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        return np.array([idx], dtype=np.int64), np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)

    w /= s
    return part.astype(np.int64), w.astype(np.float64)


def direct_top_grad(a, idx, weights, factor):
    """
    Gradient of factor * sum_k weights[k] * c_k (self factor 2, cross factor 1)
    when only top offsets idx are used.  Computed by shifts instead of FFT.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 0:
        return g

    mL = 2 * n - 1
    K = min(idx.size, weights.size)
    for i in range(K):
        wk = float(weights[i])
        if wk == 0.0:
            continue
        k = int(idx[i])
        if k < 0 or k >= mL:
            continue

        if k < n:
            lo = 0
            hi = k
        else:
            lo = k - n + 1
            hi = n - 1

        if lo > hi:
            continue

        start = k - lo
        stop_incl = k - hi
        if stop_incl > 0:
            seg = a[start:stop_incl - 1:-1]
        else:
            seg = a[start::-1]

        g[lo:hi + 1] += wk * seg

    if factor != 1.0:
        g *= factor
    return g


def power_weights_all(c, p):
    """Weights over all offsets proportional to (c / max(c))^p."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    L = int(c.size)
    if L == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        q = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        q[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return q
    r = c / cmax
    w = np.power(r, float(p))
    s = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        q = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        q[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return q
    return w / s


def weighted_full_gradient(a, c, N, p, factor):
    """Full FFT gradient for factor * sum_k q_k c_k, q from power weights."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    q = power_weights_all(c, p)
    if q.size != 2 * n - 1:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    Q = np.fft.rfft(q[::-1], N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(A * Q, N)
    start = 2 * n - 2
    stop = start + n
    if stop > conv.size:
        stop = conv.size
    g = conv[start:stop].copy()
    if g.size < n:
        g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        g[:min(n, g.size)] = conv[:min(n, g.size)]
    g *= factor
    return np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)


def rms_step(d, v):
    """Very light RMS normalization of a log-weight step direction."""
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    if d.size == 0:
        return d, v
    d = np.where(np.isfinite(d), d, 0.0)
    beta = 0.90
    v = beta * v + (1.0 - beta) * d * d
    denom = np.sqrt(v) + 1e-8
    step = d / denom
    step = np.where(np.isfinite(step), step, 0.0)
    step = np.clip(step, -10.0, 10.0)
    return step, v


def make_smooth_noise(n, rng, amp, max_m):
    """Low-frequency smooth random log-noise."""
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    max_m = max(1, min(int(max_m), n // 2 if n >= 2 else 1))
    noise = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, max_m + 1):
        am = amp / (float(m) ** 0.7)
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        noise += am * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * float(m) * t + phase)
    return noise


def random_block_shuffle(a, rng, block_size):
    """Permute contiguous blocks of a sequence."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 1 or block_size <= 1:
        return a.copy()
    block_size = max(2, int(block_size))
    preds = []
    i = 0
    while i < n:
        j = min(n, i + block_size)
        preds.append((i, j))
        i = j
    order = list(range(len(preds)))
    rng.shuffle(order)
    out = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for p in order:
        s, e = preds[p]
        out[s:e] = a[s:e]
    return out


def sparsify(a, frac):
    """Set the smallest frac fraction of coordinates to zero."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 1 or frac <= 0.0:
        return normalize(a)
    k = int(max(1, np.floor(frac * n)))
    k = min(n - 1, max(1, k))
    threshold = float(np.partition(a, n - k)[n - k])
    out = np.where(a > threshold + 1e-300, a, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        out = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        out = out / s
    return out


def resample_linear(a, m):
    """Linear resampling from length len(a) to length m."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    if n == m:
        return normalize(a)
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, float(n - 1), m, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.interp(x, np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64), a)
    return normalize(b)


def resample_tile(a, m):
    """Upsample by tiling and interpolating to exactly length m."""
    a = normalize(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    if n == m:
        return a
    if n == 1 or m < n:
        return resample_linear(a, m)
    reps = -(-m // n)
    tiled = np.tile(a, reps)
    if tiled.size > m:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, float(tiled.size - 1), m, dtype=np.float64)
        tiled = np.interp(x, np.arange(tiled.size, dtype=np.float64), tiled)
    elif tiled.size < m:
        pad = np.full(m - tiled.size, tiled[-1], dtype=np.float64)
        tiled = np.concatenate([tiled, pad])
    return normalize(tiled)


def load_parent():
    """Load the supplied state-of-the-art sequence, if present."""
    obj = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if obj is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(obj, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.size < 8:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    return arr / s


def default_parent(n, seed=0):
    """Fallback smooth random initialization."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    max_m = min(120, n // 4) if n > 4 else 1
    w = make_smooth_noise(n, rng, 0.25, max_m)
    w += 0.02 * np.asarray(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=n), dtype=np.float64)
    return softmax_weights(regularize_w(w))


def generate_candidates(parent, max_n, seed=0):
    """Create initial candidates at several lengths."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n0 = int(parent.size)
    raw_lengths = []

    for f in (0.5, 0.7, 0.8, 1.0, 1.2, 1.4, 1.7, 2.0, 2.5, 3.0):
        l = int(round(n0 * f))
        if 256 <= l <= max_n:
            raw_lengths.append(l)

    for l in (2048, 4096, 6144, 8192, 10240, 12288, 14336, 16384):
        if l <= max_n:
            raw_lengths.append(l)

    if n0 <= max_n:
        raw_lengths.append(n0)

    seen = set()
    lengths = []
    for l in raw_lengths:
        l = int(l)
        if l not in seen:
            seen.add(l)
            lengths.append(l)

    items = []
    for l in lengths[:12]:
        a1 = resample_linear(parent, l)
        a2 = resample_tile(parent, l)
        N = next_pow2(2 * l - 1)

        s1 = fft_score_only(a1, N)
        s2 = fft_score_only(a2, N)
        if s2 < s1:
            base, sbase = a2, s2
        else:
            base, sbase = a1, s1

        best_a = base
        best_s = sbase

        # Try simple entropy-changing transformations.
        base_pos = np.maximum(base, EPS)
        for pw in (0.5, 2.0):
            ac = normalize(np.power(base_pos, pw))
            sc = fft_score_only(ac, N)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s:
                best_s = sc
                best_a = ac

        if not np.isfinite(best_s):
            best_s = 2.0
            best_a = base

        items.append((float(best_s), int(l), np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).copy()))

    return items


def select_candidates(items, max_n, k=3):
    """Select a small set of lengths to optimize."""
    if not items:
        return []

    items_sorted = sorted(items, key=by_item_score)
    chosen = []
    chosen_lens = []

    for item in items_sorted:
        if len(chosen) >= k:
            break
        if item[1] not in chosen_lens:
            chosen.append(item)
            chosen_lens.append(item[1])

    best_sc = chosen[0][0] if chosen else INF

    if len(chosen) < k and items:
        largest_item = items[0]
        largest_len = items[0][1]
        for item in items:
            if item[1] > largest_len:
                largest_len = item[1]
                largest_item = item
        if largest_item[1] not in chosen_lens and by_item_score(largest_item) <= best_sc + 0.05:
            chosen.append(largest_item)
            chosen_lens.append(largest_item[1])

    for item in items_sorted:
        if len(chosen) >= k:
            break
        if item[1] not in chosen_lens:
            chosen.append(item)
            chosen_lens.append(item[1])

    return chosen


def try_mutation(a, w, score, N, n, rng, strength):
    """A few non-gradient perturbations to escape local minima."""
    best_a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    best_w = regularize_w(w)
    best_s = float(score)
    original_score = float(score)

    if n <= 1:
        return best_a, best_w, best_s, best_s < original_score - 1e-12

    max_m = min(100, n // 4) if n > 4 else 1
    if strength < 2:
        sigs = (0.01, 0.03, 0.08)
    else:
        sigs = (0.02, 0.06, 0.15, 0.30)

    for sig in sigs:
        noise = make_smooth_noise(n, rng, sig, max_m)
        wc = regularize_w(best_w + noise)
        ac = softmax_weights(wc)
        sc = fft_score_only(ac, N)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s - 1e-12:
            best_s = sc
            best_a = ac.copy()
            best_w = wc.copy()

    if strength >= 1:
        for block in (50, 150, 400):
            if n > block:
                ac = random_block_shuffle(a, rng, block)
                sc = fft_score_only(ac, N)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s - 1e-12:
                    best_s = sc
                    best_a = ac.copy()
                    best_w = log_weights(best_a)

        for frac in (0.02, 0.10):
            ac = sparsify(a, frac)
            sc = fft_score_only(ac, N)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s - 1e-12:
                best_s = sc
                best_a = ac.copy()
                best_w = log_weights(best_a)

        base_pos = np.maximum(a, EPS)
        for pw in (0.5, 2.0, 4.0):
            ac = normalize(np.power(base_pos, pw))
            sc = fft_score_only(ac, N)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s - 1e-12:
                best_s = sc
                best_a = ac.copy()
                best_w = log_weights(best_a)

    return best_a, best_w, best_s, best_s < original_score - 1e-12


def run_optimizer(a0, budget, seed=0, verbose=False):
    """Run the fixed-length optimizer within a time budget."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = float(budget)
    if budget <= 2.0:
        a = normalize(a0)
        N = next_pow2(2 * max(1, a.size) - 1)
        s, _ = fft_score_c(a, N)
        return a, s

    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, budget - 6.0)

    a = normalize(a0)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n < 8:
        a = np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64) / 4096.0
        n = 4096

    Nscore = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    Nbig = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    score, c = fft_score_c(a, Nscore)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        score, c = fft_score_c(a, Nscore)

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = score
    w = log_weights(a)

    b = a.copy()
    wb = log_weights(b)
    v_self = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v_b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Learn-rate parameters.
    base_eta = 0.05
    base_eta_b = 0.08
    no = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = t0

    # Adaptive top-K choices.
    baseK = max(4, int(round(0.0015 * n)))
    largeK = int(min(512, max(32, 0.02 * n)))
    maxK = max(4, largeK)

    K_candidates = []
    for K in (1, 2, 3, 5, 8, 16, 32, 64, 128, 256):
        if K <= maxK:
            K_candidates.append(K)
    for K in (baseK, max(1, baseK * 2), largeK):
        if K not in K_candidates and 1 <= K <= maxK:
            K_candidates.append(K)
    Klist = sorted(set(K_candidates)) if K_candidates else [4, 8, 16]

    Kb_opts = [1, 3, 5, 8, 16, 32, 64]

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 4.0:
            break

        # Safety reset.
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)) or float(a.sum()) <= 0.0:
            a = best.copy()
            score = best_score
            w = log_weights(a)
            v_self.fill(0.0)
            b = a.copy()
            wb = log_weights(b)
            v_b.fill(0.0)
            continue

        score, c = fft_score_c(a, Nscore)
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            a = best.copy()
            score = best_score
            w = log_weights(a)
            v_self.fill(0.0)
            b = a.copy()
            wb = log_weights(b)
            v_b.fill(0.0)
            continue

        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = score
            best = a.copy()

        # If we drifted badly away from the best point, return to it.
        if score > best_score + 0.02:
            a = best.copy()
            score = best_score
            w = log_weights(a)
            v_self.fill(0.0)
            b = a.copy()
            wb = log_weights(b)
            v_b.fill(0.0)
            continue

        improved = False
        use_full = (no > 40 and it % 8 == 0) or (best_score < 1.55 and it % 16 == 0)

        # ---- Self-convolution gradient update ----
        if use_full and time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
            score2, c2 = fft_score_c(a, Nbig)
            if np.isfinite(score2):
                score = score2
                c = c2
                if score < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_score = score
                    best = a.copy()
            pfull = 1.0 + min(2.0, float(no) / 100.0)
            g = weighted_full_gradient(a, c, Nbig, pfull, 2.0)
        else:
            if no < 20:
                if len(Klist) > 1:
                    K = int(rng.choice([Klist[-1], Klist[-2]]))
                else:
                    K = Klist[0]
            elif no < 100:
                if len(Klist) >= 3:
                    K = int(rng.choice(Klist[-3:]))
                else:
                    K = int(rng.choice(Klist))
            else:
                K = int(rng.choice(Klist))

            p = 1.0 + min(6.0, float(no) / 50.0)
            idx, wts = topK_weights(c, K, p)
            g = direct_top_grad(a, idx, wts, 2.0)

        g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
        mean_g = float(np.mean(g)) if g.size else 0.0
        d = n * (g - mean_g)
        d = np.where(np.isfinite(d), d, 0.0)

        if float(np.max(np.abs(d))) > 1e-14:
            step, v_self = rms_step(d, v_self)
            alphas = (base_eta, base_eta * 0.5)
            if no > 20:
                alphas = alphas + (base_eta * 2.0,)

            best_cand = None
            best_wc = None
            best_sc = score

            for alpha in alphas:
                wc = regularize_w(w - float(alpha) * step)
                ac = softmax_weights(wc)
                sc = fft_score_only(ac, Nscore)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_cand = ac.copy()
                    best_wc = wc.copy()

            if best_cand is not None:
                a = best_cand
                w = best_wc
                score = best_sc
                if score < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_score = score
                    best = a.copy()
                improved = True
                no = 0
                base_eta = min(0.5, base_eta * 1.08)
                v_self *= 0.6

        if not improved:
            base_eta = max(0.002, base_eta * 0.85)

        # ---- Cross-correlation subproblem update for auxiliary b ----
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 20.0:
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)) or float(b.sum()) <= 0.0:
                b = a.copy()
                wb = log_weights(b)
                v_b.fill(0.0)

            if no > 100 and rng.random() < 0.15:
                b = a.copy()
                wb = log_weights(b)
                v_b.fill(0.0)

            use_full_b = (no > 80 and it % 25 == 0)
            if use_full_b:
                h = fft_conv_ab(a, b, Nbig)
                pfull = 1.0 + min(3.0, float(no) / 80.0)
                gb = weighted_full_gradient(a, h, Nbig, pfull, 1.0)
            else:
                h = fft_conv_ab(a, b, Nscore)
                if no < 20:
                    Kb = int(rng.choice([16, 32]))
                elif no < 100:
                    Kb = int(rng.choice([8, 16, 32]))
                else:
                    Kb = int(rng.choice(Kb_opts))
                idxb, wibs = topK_weights(h, Kb, p)
                gb = direct_top_grad(a, idxb, wibs, 1.0)

            gb = np.where(np.isfinite(gb), gb, 0.0)
            mean_gb = float(np.mean(gb)) if gb.size else 0.0
            db = n * (gb - mean_gb)
            db = np.where(np.isfinite(db), db, 0.0)

            if h.size:
                hmax = float(np.max(h))
            else:
                hmax = 0.0
            if not np.isfinite(hmax):
                hmax = 0.0

            cross_improved = False
            best_bc = None
            best_wbc = None
            best_h = hmax

            if float(np.max(np.abs(db))) > 1e-14:
                stepb, v_b = rms_step(db, v_b)
                alphas_b = (base_eta_b, base_eta_b * 0.5)
                for alpha in alphas_b:
                    wbc = regularize_w(wb - float(alpha) * stepb)
                    bc = softmax_weights(wbc)
                    hc = fft_conv_ab(a, bc, Nscore if not use_full_b else Nbig)
                    hm = float(np.max(hc)) if hc.size else 0.0
                    if not np.isfinite(hm):
                        hm = 0.0
                    if hm < best_h - 1e-12:
                        best_h = hm
                        best_bc = bc.copy()
                        best_wbc = wbc.copy()

            # Frank-Wolfe-style jump toward the smallest weighted cross coordinate.
            if best_bc is None and no > 10:
                finite_gb = np.where(np.isfinite(gb), gb, np.inf)
                jmin = int(np.argmin(finite_gb))
                for lam in (0.5, 1.0):
                    bc = b.copy()
                    bc *= (1.0 - lam)
                    bc[jmin] = max(0.0, bc[jmin] + lam)
                    s = float(bc.sum())
                    if s > 0.0:
                        bc = bc / s
                    hc = fft_conv_ab(a, bc, Nscore if not use_full_b else Nbig)
                    hm = float(np.max(hc)) if hc.size else 0.0
                    if not np.isfinite(hm):
                        hm = 0.0
                    if hm < best_h - 1e-12:
                        best_h = hm
                        best_bc = bc.copy()
                        best_wbc = log_weights(best_bc)

            if best_bc is not None:
                b = best_bc
                wb = best_wbc
                cross_improved = True
                base_eta_b = min(0.5, base_eta_b * 1.08)
                v_b *= 0.6
            else:
                base_eta_b = max(0.002, base_eta_b * 0.85)

        # ---- Mix current a with optimized b ----
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 15.0:
            mix_improved = False
            best_mix_a = None
            best_mix_w = None
            best_mix_score = score

            # Linear mixes.
            for tval in (0.30, 0.70):
                ca = (1.0 - tval) * a + tval * b
                s = float(ca.sum())
                if s > 0.0:
                    if abs(s - 1.0) > 1e-12:
                        ca = ca / s
                    sc = fft_score_only(ca, Nscore)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_mix_score - 1e-12:
                        best_mix_score = sc
                        best_mix_a = ca.copy()
                        best_mix_w = log_weights(best_mix_a)

            # Geometric/log mixes.
            log_mixs = (0.50,)
            if no > 30:
                log_mixs = (0.50, 1.00)
            for tval in log_mixs:
                wc = regularize_w((1.0 - tval) * w + tval * wb)
                ca = softmax_weights(wc)
                sc = fft_score_only(ca, Nscore)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_mix_score - 1e-12:
                    best_mix_score = sc
                    best_mix_a = ca.copy()
                    best_mix_w = wc.copy()

            if best_mix_a is not None and best_mix_score < score - 1e-12:
                a = best_mix_a
                w = best_mix_w
                score = best_mix_score
                if score < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_score = score
                    best = a.copy()
                improved = True
                no = 0
                base_eta = min(0.5, base_eta * 1.03)
                v_self *= 0.5
                v_b *= 0.5

        if not improved:
            no += 1

        # ---- Mutations / restarts ----
        if no > 50 and (no % 50 == 0 or no > 400):
            if score > best_score + 0.005:
                a = best.copy()
                score = best_score
                w = log_weights(a)
                v_self.fill(0.0)
                b = a.copy()
                wb = log_weights(b)
                v_b.fill(0.0)
                no = 25
                continue

            strength = 1 if no < 200 else 2
            new_a, new_w, new_s, mut_ok = try_mutation(a, w, score, Nscore, n, rng, strength)
            if mut_ok:
                a = new_a
                w = new_w
                score = new_s
                if score < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_score = score
                    best = a.copy()
                improved = True
                no = 0
                v_self.fill(0.0)
                v_b.fill(0.0)
                b = a.copy()
                wb = log_weights(b)
            elif no > 400:
                a = best.copy()
                score = best_score
                w = log_weights(a)
                v_self.fill(0.0)
                b = a.copy()
                wb = log_weights(b)
                v_b.fill(0.0)
                no = 0
                improved = True

        if verbose and time.monotonic() - last_print > 20.0:
            print(
                f"[opt] n={n} it={it} current={score:.6f} "
                f"best={best_score:.6f} no={no} elapsed={time.monotonic() - t0:.1f}"
            )
            last_print = time.monotonic()

    return best, best_score


def exact_score_norm(a):
    """Exact O(n^2) score for a normalized nonnegative sequence."""
    a = normalize(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return INF
    if n > 25000:
        # Avoid excessively slow exact convolution for unexpectedly long sequences.
        return fft_score_only(a, next_pow2(2 * n - 1))
    b = np.convolve(a, a)
    if b.size == 0:
        return INF
    mx = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return INF
    return float(2.0 * n * mx)


def finalize_sequence(a):
    """Convert normalized search result to final evaluator-safe heights."""
    a = normalize(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return [0.0]

    scale = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * n))
    a = a * scale
    np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0, out=a)

    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        a = a * (scale / float(n))
    else:
        a = a * (scale / s)

    np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0, out=a)

    s2 = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s2) or s2 < 0.01:
        if s2 <= 0.0:
            a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) * (scale / float(n))
        else:
            a = a * (0.01 / s2)
        np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0, out=a)

    return [float(x) for x in a]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Entry point required by the harness.

    Runs the hybrid FFT/local-search optimizer on several candidate lengths
    derived from the supplied parent construction, then returns the best
    exact-validated sequence found.
    """
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    budget = min(max(1.0, budget), 2390.0)

    t0 = time.monotonic()

    parent = load_parent()
    if parent is None:
        parent = default_parent(4096, int(seed))

    max_n = 16000
    if int(parent.size) > max_n:
        parent = resample_linear(parent, max_n)

    items = generate_candidates(parent, max_n, int(seed))
    selected = select_candidates(items, max_n, k=3)

    if not selected:
        return finalize_sequence(parent)

    # Allocate most of the time to the best initial candidate, but still explore
    # one or two alternatives, especially longer lengths.
    total = max(1.0, budget - 45.0)
    if len(selected) == 1:
        fracs = [1.0]
    elif len(selected) == 2:
        fracs = [0.72, 0.28]
    else:
        fracs = [0.62, 0.26, 0.12]

    results = []
    for i, item in enumerate(selected):
        sc0, l0, arr0 = item
        btime = fracs[i] * total
        if btime < 5.0:
            continue
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > budget - 25.0:
            break

        print(
            f"phase {i + 1}/{len(selected)} length={l0} "
            f"initial={sc0:.6f} phase_budget={btime:.1f}"
        )
        best_arr, best_sc = run_optimizer(
            arr0, btime, seed=int(seed) + 101 * i, verbose=(i == 0)
        )
        if best_arr is not None and best_arr.size > 0:
            results.append((np.asarray(best_arr, dtype=np.float64).copy(), float(best_sc), int(l0)))

    if not results:
        sc0, l0, arr0 = selected[0]
        results.append((np.asarray(arr0, dtype=np.float64).copy(), float(sc0), int(l0)))

    # Exact validation of the retained candidates.
    best_final = None
    best_final_val = INF
    for arr, sc, _ in results:
        val = exact_score_norm(arr)
        if np.isfinite(val):
            if val < best_final_val:
                best_final_val = val
                best_final = arr
        else:
            if sc < best_final_val:
                best_final_val = sc
                best_final = arr

    if best_final is None:
        best_final = results[0][0]

    # Optional short polish if much time remains and we have not reached the target.
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
    remaining = budget - elapsed
    if remaining > 90.0 and best_final_val > 1.5027:
        polish_budget = min(remaining - 20.0, 350.0)
        if polish_budget > 10.0:
            print(f"polish length={best_final.size} budget={polish_budget:.1f}")
            polish_arr, polish_sc = run_optimizer(
                normalize(best_final), polish_budget, seed=int(seed) + 999, verbose=False
            )
            polish_val = exact_score_norm(polish_arr)
            if np.isfinite(polish_val) and polish_val < best_final_val:
                best_final = polish_arr
                best_final_val = polish_val

    return finalize_sequence(best_final)
```
